- **flight**: Add production flight checks to a Node server (record the steps of its long-running work, check them against approved promises, report findings), or read its flight report ([mindmakr/reins](https://github.com/mindmakr/reins/blob/HEAD/commands/flight.md))
- **flight-archive**: Move old flight workbench files into a timestamped archive bundle. ([DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable](https://github.com/DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable/blob/HEAD/commands/flight-archive.md))
- **flight-booking-component**: Create a flight booking component ([jonniebigodes/mock-design-system-ark-ui-mcp](https://github.com/jonniebigodes/mock-design-system-ark-ui-mcp/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/flight-booking-component.md))
- **flight-check**: Flight Check — offline safety scan for a skill, CLAUDE.md or prompt (no account, no AI cost) ([ModelBound/modelbound-claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/ModelBound/modelbound-claude-code-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/flight-check.md))
- **flight-cleanup**: Strip closed and stale tasks from the flight open-task list, archiving what was removed. ([DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable](https://github.com/DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable/blob/HEAD/commands/flight-cleanup.md))
- **flight-commands**: `sf takeoff`・`sf land` 等、WiFi CLI 経由でフライトコマンドを StampFly に送信し、リアルタイムで 監視するトップレベルコマンド群（それぞれ独立したコマンドで、`sf flight &lt;command&gt;` という サブコマンドではない）。 ([M5Fly-kanazawa/stampfly_ecosystem](https://github.com/M5Fly-kanazawa/stampfly_ecosystem/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/flight-commands.md) · ★51)
- **flight-engineer**: Plain-language project-health sweep — keeps a project airworthy, fixes the safe stuff for you, gates merges/deploys on your approval ([Robot-Friends-Community/airport-authority](https://github.com/Robot-Friends-Community/airport-authority/blob/HEAD/commands/flight-engineer.md))
- **flight-help**: Explain what flight is and how to use it. ([DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable](https://github.com/DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable/blob/HEAD/commands/flight-help.md))
- **flight-land**: Close the flight session — write a summary to history and carry forward unresolved tasks. ([DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable](https://github.com/DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable/blob/HEAD/commands/flight-land.md))
- **flight-memo**: Capture an open task or a longer memo into the flight workbench. ([DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable](https://github.com/DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable/blob/HEAD/commands/flight-memo.md))
- **flight-plan**: Chart work into flight-ready issues with dependencies mapped. ([SpiceLabsHQ/Reaper](https://github.com/SpiceLabsHQ/Reaper/blob/HEAD/commands/flight-plan.md))

- **flight-stilcheck**: Check text against the flight style profile, report deviations, and optionally produce a revised version. ([DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable](https://github.com/DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable/blob/HEAD/commands/flight-stilcheck.md))
- **flight-unlock**: Write a permissive permissions file so future sessions skip approval prompts (Claude Code only). ([DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable](https://github.com/DigitalLeadershipAG/flight-portable/blob/HEAD/commands/flight-unlock.md))
- **flightdeck**: Put this whole session under FlightDeck governance — every task packeted, dispatched, and recorded as you work ([NorthGate-Strategic-LLC/flightdeck-plugin](https://github.com/NorthGate-Strategic-LLC/flightdeck-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/flightdeck.md) · ★1)

- **flipper**: Enters strict Flipper mode. Explicitly reminds you of the multi-agent system rules (CLAUDE.md) before processing the task $ARGUMENTS. ([samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0](https://github.com/samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/flipper.md) · ★4)
- **flipper**: Drive Alex's Flipper Zero from the CLI -- read/write files, analyze captures, transmit, over USB or Bluetooth. ([BaesTheorem/exobrain-harness](https://github.com/BaesTheorem/exobrain-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/flipper.md) · ★1)
- **flipper-council**: Forces the convening of the Tripartite Council over $ARGUMENTS, even if the master would not consider it necessary on its own. Useful when the user wants explicit adversarial deliberation. ([samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0](https://github.com/samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/flipper-council.md) · ★4)
- **flipper-disable**: Disable Flipper Zero permission routing ([alliecatowo/claude-flip](https://github.com/alliecatowo/claude-flip/blob/HEAD/commands/flipper-disable.md) · ★1)
- **flipper-enable**: Enable Flipper Zero permission routing ([alliecatowo/claude-flip](https://github.com/alliecatowo/claude-flip/blob/HEAD/commands/flipper-enable.md) · ★1)
- **flipper-quick**: Shortcut - skips the Council for a task $ARGUMENTS that does NOT match the G3 list. Only valid if the automatic irreversibility check comes back negative. Goes straight to the master deciding or to the domain specialist. ([samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0](https://github.com/samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/flipper-quick.md) · ★4)
- **flipper-redirect**: Fixes routing at runtime when the master delegated to the wrong specialist or a specialist responded with out_of_scope. $ARGUMENTS is the name of the correct specialist to reassign the task to. ([samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0](https://github.com/samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/flipper-redirect.md) · ★4)
- **flipper-reset**: Clears the master's conversational context when the user suspects severe contamination (decisions affected by irrelevant history). User opt-in; NOT invoked automatically. Does NOT destroy any files on disk — it only signals to the master that it must operate as if the conversational history did not exist. ([samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0](https://github.com/samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/flipper-reset.md) · ★4)
- **flipper-review-wildcards**: Lists the ad-hoc Council wildcards that have not yet been promoted to the closed catalog of angles. Useful for deciding whether a recurring wildcard deserves to be formalized as a stable angle. User opt-in, not scheduled. ([samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0](https://github.com/samartined/My-Momentum-Firmware-F0/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/flipper-review-wildcards.md) · ★4)
- **flipper-risk**: Toggle risk assessment display on Flipper ([alliecatowo/claude-flip](https://github.com/alliecatowo/claude-flip/blob/HEAD/commands/flipper-risk.md) · ★1)
- **flipper-status**: Check Flipper Zero connection status ([alliecatowo/claude-flip](https://github.com/alliecatowo/claude-flip/blob/HEAD/commands/flipper-status.md) · ★1)
- **flipper-test**: Test Flipper Zero connection with a test message ([alliecatowo/claude-flip](https://github.com/alliecatowo/claude-flip/blob/HEAD/commands/flipper-test.md) · ★1)
- **flips**: Trump position reversals and flip-flops in the last 7 days ([alsgur9865-sketch/claude-trump](https://github.com/alsgur9865-sketch/claude-trump/blob/HEAD/commands/flips.md) · ★1)
